**Orchestrator rulings on NR-P1 … NR-P6 (2026-11-09, draft 1.1, before Step 0b):**
- **NR-P1: (a) the stack** — three specialists (rule-engine, backend-infrastructure, payroll-integration) stacked and merged once, as drafted; it keeps the specialist boundaries the cross-domain convention exists for (`docs/AGENTS.md` § Cross-Domain Authorization). (Its single test pass became two in draft 1.3, NR-P9.)
- **NR-P2: the lean** — an interval open towards the past starts at the Monday of the earliest exported month's opening week. **Amended by NR-P12 (draft 1.3):** that start, and "no exported month, no call", govern the exported-month call only; the settled-year call is always made.
- **NR-P3: the proposal** — Teamoversigt counts as slow when the 300-person median more than doubles or exceeds 2 s; either becomes an F-series row in the performance register.
- **NR-P4: build it, in TASK-14838** — the edit drawer's organisation change with a future agreement date fails as a whole only because S148's new "no future transfer date" rule meets the drawer's single date; a regression this sprint creates is never an extra (the SMALLEST COMPLETE CHANGE block). Drafted in draft 1.2: TASK-14838's row and files, its order after 14837, the `PersonDrawer.tsx` shared-files row, AC-C13j.
- **NR-P5: the lean** — the six Docker-gated pins (eight from draft 1.3: M-14, M-15; from draft 1.4.1 nine mutations over eight AC ids, M-16 a second mutation on AC-C2p; from "final + 6" eleven over eight, M-22 and M-23 two more on AC-S0-2e) prove RED in the four CI mutation runs (five from "final + 6": CI-M2b, Orchestrator ruling 2026-11-11) (`docs/AGENTS.md` § RED evidence: a named mutation at the wave gate; Docker pins can only run in CI). **How the runs execute (the workflow patch, the frozen list, the judging): NR-P25, draft 1.5.**
- **NR-P6: the lean** — a day no record covers uses that day's OK version (version-correct); BC-P10's switch of Teamoversigt to each day's OK version is told to the owner in the sprint log (invisible on seeded data).

- **NR-P1 — T14b as a three-implementer stack merged as one (DV-2).** *Options:* (a) the stack (rule-engine → payroll-integration → backend-infrastructure → test-qa → ux), one merge; (b) one `payroll-integration` agent with a four-domain extension. *Trade-off:* (a) gives each domain its specialist and smaller reviews, at the cost of three sequential dispatches and a GATE only at the tip; (b) is one dispatch and one context, but the sprint's largest change in one agent. **Lean (a).**
- **NR-P2 — the `EffectiveFrom` of an employment-date interval open towards the past (N-1).** *Options:* (a) the Monday of the opening week of the employee's earliest exported month (the meaning the field documents, `HrBackdateWorklistRepository.cs:39-56`); (b) that month's first day; (c) `0001-01-01`. *Trade-off:* (a) is exact and matches `PRIOR_MONTH_REOPENED`; (b) reads more simply to HR but can sit a few days after the first day the change reaches; (c) is meaningless on screen. **Lean (a).**
- **NR-P3 — when the Teamoversigt timing counts as slow (N-3).** *Proposal:* the 300-person median after T14b more than twice its median before, or above 2 seconds, becomes an F-series row; otherwise the NOTE is refuted. *Alternative:* register any measurable increase. **Lean: the proposal** (CI timings are noisy; a doubling is a signal, a few percent is not).
- **NR-P4 — the drawer's future date with an organisation change (Found while drafting).** *Options:* (a) register a usability QUAL row and build nothing; (b) a small `ux` change in wave 6: while the organisation field is changed, the drawer caps its date at today and says why (folded into TASK-14838's scope, which then also touches `frontend/src/hooks/useEditPerson.ts` and the drawer's date picker; no file shared with 14832–14837). *Trade-off:* (a) costs nothing now and leaves HR a 400 with `nearestValid` = today; (b) costs a small task and applies the principle NR-15 set for "Arkiver" (a screen never offers an action the server refuses). **Lean (b).** Not drafted into the ledger until ruled.
- **NR-P5 — Docker N pins proven by CI mutation evidence runs** (CI-M2, CI-M4a, CI-M4b, CI-M5: four extra runs). *Options:* (a) the runs; (b) S147's "argued in the docstring" only. *Trade-off:* (a) actually observes each RED (`docs/AGENTS.md:177-186` is the rule; fee2a635's evidence PR #52 is the mechanism's precedent) at about four CI runs; (b) is free but leaves six pins (eight from draft 1.3) whose RED nobody saw. **Lean (a).**
- **NR-P6 — the OK version for a day no record covers (N-2, BC-P10).** *Options:* (a) day d's OK version, as for every other day of the dated lookup and as the balance does since S137; (b) the users row's live OK version, today's Teamoversigt source. *Trade-off:* (a) is version-correct and keeps one rule per screen; (b) keeps today's Teamoversigt figure for a record-less employee in a past OK version even where the two versions' norms differ. Both give today's figure on seeded data. **Lean (a).**
- **NR-P15 (raised while folding in draft 1.3; ruled (a) in 1.3.1, see the rulings above) — M-14 and M-15's override half cannot share CI-M4a.** *In plain words:* a CI mutation run applies all its mutations at once, so they must not hide each other (the mutation list's rule: "Mutations batched into one run are independent of each other's facts"). M-14 makes the override read return the head row; under it AC-C2p (2), "an override edited twice on one date resolves the later write", still passes, because the head row already holds the later write. With both in CI-M4a, M-15's frozen list would show a listed fact green: a false finding, and that part's RED never observed. *Options:* (a) run M-14 in CI-M4b beside M-10 (M-10 breaks the agreement-config seed path and AC-C2n's facts; M-14 breaks the override read and AC-C2o's; neither touches the other's facts); (b) keep M-14 in CI-M4a and move M-15's override half to CI-M4b; (c) keep both in CI-M4a and take AC-C2p (2) off M-15's frozen list, so its RED is seen nowhere. *Trade-off:* (a) and (b) both keep four runs and observe every RED; (a) keeps M-15's read half in one run; (c) leaves an N fact unproved. **Lean (a).** Ruled (a) in 1.3.1: M-14 at CI-M4b.

| final + 27 | 2026-11-19 | Planning Drafter (Opus 5.5), for the Orchestrator (Opus 5.5 seat) | **The plan amendments of the S148 wave-5 batch-D brief adjudication** (`.claude/reviews/S148-wave5D-briefs-adjudication.md`, Fable 5.2, RULED-WITH-QUESTIONS; its "Plan amendments ('final + 27')", items 1–10), applied together with the five briefs' draft 2 (TASK-14830, -T, 14831, -T, 14846, each "adjudicated"). **Its one owner question (§ 2: should the replay promise keep its one known exception for wage-type codes?) does not block dispatch; it is recorded in O-19 as amendment 9 words it, and the Orchestrator records the answer separately.** *In plain words:* the five briefs of batch D (the month's proof holding its rules by value, its tests, payroll's check under the pay-inputs lock, its tests, the edit drawer's self lock) were judged sound and can dispatch once corrected. Payroll's check is right as designed: it re-reads the month's inputs on separate connections, and since no change can commit while it holds the lock, those reads are stable. The price is availability, not correctness: each recording needs a second database connection while it holds its first, so a pile-up as large as the connection pool would stall and fail, writing nothing; that is registered (SEC-066), marked in the code and measured by a fourth figure, not fixed here. The wage-type mapping stays out of the check, because the gap it leaves is not a race a lock could close; whether to keep that exception in the replay promise is the owner's question. Two test claims were corrected (one mutation, M-7, could not trip the arm it was said to; a second, M-52, now does), a round-trip test of the stored rules gained two arms and a mutation (M-51) that makes it provable, and the drawer also locks two "carry forward" checkboxes that would otherwise sit there inert. **Carried:** (1) TASK-14831's row `:369` (the label `Payroll Integration (extended into Infrastructure PayInputsLock.cs:49-51, comment only, cross-domain authorized)`;
